Motivation: 热红外（IR）航拍车辆检测在实际应用中至关重要，但真实IR数据稀缺且采集成本高。虽然RGB航拍数据丰富，RGB到IR的跨模态翻译面临热特征（如发动机热量）不可观测的难题。作者旨在探索现代生成式翻译器能否克服这种跨模态鸿沟，利用丰富的UAV RGB数据生成合成IR数据来改善目标域上的红外车辆检测性能。

Method: 研究使用配对RGB-IR源域数据集训练多种生成式翻译器，包括监督GAN、基于ControlNet的扩散模型以及通过LoRA进行基础模型编辑。翻译器应用于留出目标数据集中的RGB训练图像生成合成IR图像。随后使用这些合成IR数据训练RF-DETR车辆检测器，并在五个航拍数据集的未见IR测试集上进行评估，其中Kust4K和VTUAV作为目标域，评估还包括通过多种子生成和提示词变化来增加输出多样性。

Result: 合成IR数据在所有实验中一致优于RGB和灰度基线。Stable Diffusion 3.5配合ControlNet获得最佳结果：与仅使用源域IR数据训练的模型相比，Kust4K上的mAP从50.8提升到60.1，VTUAV上从25.6提升到38.4。多种子生成带来+1.1 mAP的提升，提示词变化带来+3.3 mAP的额外收益（在VTUAV上）。

Conclusion: 虽然与真实目标域IR数据相比仍存在性能差距，但生成式RGB-to-IR翻译有效缓解了IR数据稀缺问题，显著改善了跨域航拍车辆检测性能。该研究验证了现代生成模型在弥补跨模态数据采集成本方面的实用价值。

Abstract: Synthetic training data is crucial for developing vision AI when real-world data is scarce, as in thermal infrared (IR) aerial vehicle detection. While abundant UAV RGB imagery motivates RGB-to-IR translation for data augmentation, unobservable thermal traits (e.g., engine heat) make learning transferable mappings challenging. This work investigates whether modern generative translators can overcome this cross-modal gap to improve infrared vehicle detection on unseen UAV target domains. Translators are trained on paired RGB-IR source datasets and applied to RGB training images from held-out target datasets to generate synthetic IR data. Evaluated methods include supervised GANs, ControlNet-based diffusion models, and foundation-model editing via LoRA. The resulting synthetic IR imagery is used to train RF-DETR vehicle detectors, which are evaluated on unseen IR target test splits across five aerial datasets, with Kust4K and VTUAV serving as target domains. Synthetic IR consistently outperforms RGB and grayscale baselines. Stable Diffusion 3.5 with ControlNet yields the best results, improving mAP from 50.8 to 60.1 on Kust4K and from 25.6 to 38.4 on VTUAV compared to models trained only on source-domain IR data. Increasing output diversity via multiple seeds (+1.1 mAP) and prompt variations (+3.3 mAP) provides additional gains on VTUAV. Although a performance gap to real target IR data remains, generative RGB-to-IR translation effectively mitigates IR data scarcity and improves cross-domain aerial vehicle detection.

</details>


### [90] [Stereo 4D Radar for 3D Object Detection: Integrating Geometric Alignment and Absolute Velocity Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02560)
*Seung-Hyun Song,Dong-Hee Paek,Woong-Chan Byun,Seung-Hyun Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于立体4D雷达的3D物体检测框架，利用左右雷达之间的几何视差估计物体的绝对速度，并融合互补特征实现更稳健的感知。在室内自建立体4D雷达数据集上的实验表明，相比最先进的单目4D雷达基线，AP 3D提升8.82点，AP BEV提升9.0点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D雷达原始信号包含大量杂波（路面、护栏、周围车辆）、多径虚影反射及接收机噪声底，预处理去噪后数据过于稀疏；且4D雷达提供的多普勒测量仅描述物体速度的径向分量，无法恢复完整运动状态。这限制了4D雷达在3D物体检测中的性能。

Method: 提出立体4D雷达3D物体检测框架，核心思想是利用左右两个雷达之间的几何视差（disparity）来估计物体的绝对速度（而非仅径向速度），并通过融合左右雷达的互补特征实现更稳健的感知。框架在自建的立体4D雷达数据集上构建和验证。

Result: 在室内自建立体4D雷达数据集上，相比最先进的单目4D雷达基线，AP 3D提升8.82点，AP BEV提升9.0点。

Conclusion: 绝对速度估计结合立体几何感知的特征融合能够显著提升3D物体检测性能，证明立体4D雷达配置是克服单目4D雷达数据稀疏与运动信息不完整问题的有效途径。

Abstract: Four-dimensional (4D) Radar is a powerful sensing modality capable of detecting surrounding three-dimensional (3D) objects under diverse weather conditions and providing Doppler-based motion information. However, raw 4D Radar signals contain significant clutter from road surfaces, guardrails, and surrounding vehicles, along with multipath-induced ghost reflections and the receiver's inherent noise floor. Consequently, preprocessing algorithms designed to remove such invalid measurements often make the Radar data excessively sparse. Moreover, the Doppler measurements provided by 4D Radar describe only the radial component of an object's velocity, limiting their ability to recover the full motion state. In this paper, we introduce a stereo 4D Radar-based 3D object detection framework that exploits the geometric disparity between left and right Radars to estimate the absolute velocity of objects and achieve more robust perception through the fusion of their complementary features. The effectiveness of the proposed framework is validated on our in-house stereo 4D Radar dataset, demonstrating performance gains of 8.82 points in AP 3D and 9.0 points in AP BEV over state-of-the-art mono 4D Radar baselines. These results demonstrate that absolute velocity estimation combined with stereo geometry-aware feature fusion leads to substantial improvements in 3D object detection.

</details>


### [91] [MARS: What Retrieval Signals Are Hidden in Multimodal Large Language Models for Text-Video Retrieval?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02565)
*Uicheol Jung,Juyoung Hong,Geuntaek Lim,Yukyung Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MARS是一个用于文本-视频检索的多层多槽位嵌入框架，通过聚合不同解码器层隐藏状态构建多个自适应表示槽位，并引入硬负样本感知的槽位专门化目标，在四个基准上达到SOTA效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期多模态大语言模型虽被改编为嵌入模型用于文本-视频检索，但往往仅用最终层的单一token来表示输入，这会压缩多样化的视频-文本线索到单一向量中，限制了细粒度检索能力，尤其在区分具有相似场景、动作和时间模式的视频时存在局限。

Method: MARS通过结合不同解码器层的隐藏状态构建多个自适应表示槽位，将文本和视频的对应槽位逐一比较并聚合相似度用于检索；同时引入硬负样本感知的槽位专门化目标函数，鼓励不同槽位捕获判别性的匹配线索。

Result: 在四个文本-视频检索基准上的实验表明，MARS在直接基于相似度的检索和重排序两种设置下均取得了目前最优的结果；消融实验验证了多层融合、多槽位和硬负样本感知的槽位专门化三者提供了互补性增益。代码已开源。

Conclusion: MARS通过多层与多槽位的嵌入设计有效克服了单一token表示的信息瓶颈，能够更精细地捕捉视频与文本间的多样匹配线索，为基于多模态大语言模型的文本-视频检索提供了一种更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Text-video retrieval requires representations that can distinguish videos with similar scenes, actions, and temporal patterns. Recent multimodal large language models have been adapted as embedding models, but they often represent each input using a single token from the final layer. This can compress diverse video-text cues into a single vector and limit fine-grained retrieval. To address this limitation, we propose MARS, a multi-layer and multi-slot embedding framework for text-video retrieval. MARS constructs multiple adaptive representation slots by combining hidden states from different decoder layers, compares corresponding text and video slots, and aggregates their similarities for retrieval. To better handle confusing candidates, we further introduce a hard-negative-aware slot specialization objective that encourages the slots to capture discriminative matching cues. Experiments on four text-video retrieval benchmarks show that MARS achieves state-of-the-art results in both direct similarity-based retrieval and reranking settings. Ablation studies and analyses demonstrate that multi-layer fusion, multiple slots, and hard-negative-aware slot specialization provide complementary gains. Code is available at https://github.com/sejong-rcv/MARS.

</details>


### [92] [Deeply Interleaved Text-Image Contexts for Multimodal LLMs Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02573)
*Zihao Wang,Xi Xiang,Yuwen Sun,Yingyu Li,Yabo Zhang,Yihan Zeng,Fan Li,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TIC-Bench基准，专门用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在深度交错文本-图像场景中将文本和图像线索整合并恢复事实的能力。该基准涵盖逻辑、时间、空间三类核心关联，细分为8个具体类型共2280个问题。评测了10个SOTA MLLMs，发现其与人类专家存在显著性能差距，且在整合分布在不同视觉和文本输入中的证据方面存在持续困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态模型的评测和训练主要聚焦于多图像任务，忽略了交错文本与图像的真实场景。在多图像任务中，文本通常仅作为任务指令，缺乏与视觉内容的深层语义交互；而实际应用（如文图共同创作、角色追踪、空间重建）要求文本与图像持续交互。因此需要建立一个基准去评估模型在这种深度交错上下文中的理解与整合能力。

Method: 作者构建了一个新型基准TIC-Bench，设计包含逻辑关联、时间关联和空间关联三个核心领域，进一步细分为八个具体任务类型，共2280个问题。基准要求模型整合文本与图像线索并恢复地面真值事实，随后在10个先进MLLM上与人类专家进行对比评测。

Result: 评测结果显示，10个SOTA MLLMs相比人类专家存在显著的性能差距，模型在整合分布于交错视觉和文本输入的证据方面表现出持续性困难。

Conclusion: TIC-Bench提供了一个有价值的分析工具，用于评估和推动多模态模型在深度交错文本-图像场景中有效整合图文信息的能力。

Abstract: Current evaluations and training of multimodal models predominantly focus on multi-image tasks, largely overlooking interleaved text-image scenarios. In such multi-image tasks, text typically serves merely as task instructions, lacking deep semantic interaction with the visual content. In contrast, realworld applications like text-image co-creation, character tracking, and spatial reconstruction require constant interaction between text and images. Consequently, models must possess a deep understanding of these interleaved contexts. To bridge this gap, we introduce a novel benchmark, TIC-Bench (deeply interleaved Text-Image Contexts), designed to evaluate the capability of models to integrate text-image clues and recover the ground truth facts within deeply interleaved contexts. This benchmark encompasses three core domains: Logical, Temporal, and Spatial Association, which are further categorized into eight specific types, comprising a total of 2,280 questions. We evaluated 10 state-of-the-art MLLMs and observed a substantial performance gap compared to human experts, together with persistent difficulties in integrating evidence distributed across interleaved visual and textual inputs. Ultimately, this benchmark provides a valuable analytical tool for assessing and advancing the ability of multimodal models to effectively integrate text and image information in deeply interleaved contexts. TIC-Bench is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/pino10010/TIC-Bench

</details>


### [93] [RVSD: Retrieval Vision Sparse Decoding for Mitigating Visual Hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02731)
*Canjie Liu,Jiawen Kang,Jinbo Wen,Zishao Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RVSD（检索视觉稀疏解码）框架，一种无需训练、即插即用的解码框架，在同一解码过程中首次统一了token稀疏化与语义空间视觉检索（SSVR），以缓解大型视觉语言模型中的视觉幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型在视觉语言任务中表现优异，但仍易产生视觉幻觉（VHs），影响其在真实应用中的可靠性。现有解决方案通常需要人工整理的数据集、额外的训练或多次迭代解码，带来较高的计算开销。

Method: RVSD包含两个核心组件：（1）语义导向的token选择策略，选择性稀疏化冗余token同时保留关键视觉信息；（2）语义空间视觉检索（SSVR）机制，将视觉补偿重构为共享语义空间中的按需跨模态检索过程。整个框架无需训练，即插即用，在单一解码过程中完成。

Result: 大量实验表明，RVSD在缓解视觉幻觉方面达到了最先进（SOTA）的性能，并在长上下文生成场景下保持稳健的抑制能力。

Conclusion: RVSD通过训练自由、即插即用的解码框架有效实现视觉幻觉抑制，兼顾性能与计算效率，且代码已开源。

Abstract: Large vision-language models have achieved remarkable success in vision-language tasks. However, they remain prone to Visual Hallucinations (VHs), undermining their reliability in real-world applications. Existing solutions typically require curated datasets, additional training, or multi-round decoding, resulting in considerable computational overhead. In this paper, we propose \textbf{RVSD} (\underline{R}etrieval \underline{V}ision \underline{S}parse \underline{D}ecoding), a training-free and plug-and-play decoding framework that, for the first time, unifies token sparsification and \textbf{Semantic-Space Visual Retrieval} (SSVR) within a single decoding pass. Within RVSD, we introduce a \textbf{semantics-directed token selection} strategy that selectively sparsifies redundant tokens while preserving critical visual information. We further propose the SSVR mechanism, which reformulates visual compensation as an on-demand cross-modal retrieval process within a shared semantic space. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RVSD achieves state-of-the-art performance in mitigating VHs while maintaining robust suppression capabilities under long-context generation settings. Our code is available here.\footnote{https://github.com/canjie-liu/RVSD}

</details>


### [94] [Generalizable Brain Tumor Segmentation with Self-Training and Tumor-Aware Deformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02600)
*Henrique Zan Grande,Jeovane Honorio Alves,Rayson Laroca,Andre Gustavo Hochuli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对BraTS 2026挑战赛GoAT任务的方法，基于nnU-Net框架集成大残差编码器、半监督伪标签学习与肿瘤感知可变形增强，以提升跨异质人群的脑肿瘤子区域分割鲁棒性。提交配置在验证集上所有肿瘤区域均优于仅标注基线上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑肿瘤分割需要应对异质患者群体的泛化挑战，BraTS-GoAT任务要求模型在多样化的患者人群中实现稳健的肿瘤亚区分割。仅依赖标注数据训练的方法在不同影像分布下性能有限，需要结合未标注数据与有效的正则化/增强策略来提升跨人群泛化能力。

Method: 采用nnU-Net框架配合大型残差编码器作为骨干网络；利用未标注训练数据生成伪标签实现半监督自训练；引入肿瘤感知的可变形增强方法，在局部形变病灶的同时保留周围正常解剖结构；并系统评估各组件单独及组合贡献，同时探索不同比例高置信伪标签样本的影响。

Result: 在BraTS-GoAT验证集上，Whole Tumor、Tumor Core和Enhancing Tumor的Dice/NSD分别达到0.881/0.473、0.817/0.490和0.775/0.533，所有肿瘤区域均超越仅用标注数据的基线，验证了自训练与所提出增强方法的互补性。

Conclusion: 半监督伪标签自训练与肿瘤感知可变形增强在nnU-Net大残差编码器架构下具有互补增益，能有效提升跨肿瘤异质人群的分割泛化能力，且代码已开源供复现与进一步研究。

Abstract: This work presents an approach to the Generalizability Across Tumors (BraTS-GoAT) task of the BraTS 2026 Challenge, which focuses on robust segmentation of brain tumor sub-regions across a heterogeneous patient population. The proposed method employs the nnU-Net framework with a large residual encoder architecture, integrating a semi-supervised learning technique with pseudo-labels generated from the unlabeled training data and a tumor-aware deformable augmentation that locally deforms the lesion while preserving the surrounding anatomy. We evaluate the individual contributions of each component, as well as their combination, using varying proportions of the most confident pseudo-labeled cases. The submitted configuration for the generalization task achieves Dice and NSD scores of 0.881 and 0.473 for Whole Tumor, 0.817 and 0.490 for Tumor Core, and 0.775 and 0.533 for Enhancing Tumor on the BraTS-GoAT validation set, improving over the labeled-only baselines across all tumor regions and confirming that self-training and the proposed augmentation are complementary. Our source code is publicly available at https://github.com/Henrique-zan/brats-goat-2026/.

</details>


### [95] [AffectDelta: Beyond Emotion Labels for Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02616)
*Xingzu Zhan,Lin Gu,Ruogu Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AffectDelta，一种源感知的情绪驱动图像编辑方法。与传统依赖单一情绪类别和文本指令的方法不同，AffectDelta将编辑视为八维情绪分布之间的转移。它使用冻结的情绪分布预测器估计源图像状态，并通过有符号的源到目标差值编码转移方向与幅度。内部转移编码器和源感知扩散骨干共同协作，将信号转换为上下文相关的语义与外观变化。作者构建了AffectPair-249K数据集（248,841对图像），涵盖跨类别和类内情绪转移，实验表明其在情感对齐和内容保留上优于六个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情绪驱动图像编辑方法存在两个核心局限：(1) 场景级编辑器通常使用单一情绪类别作为目标，但真实情绪往往是混合的，将多共存的情绪压缩为一个主导标签会损失细粒度的情感信息；(2) 基于文本操作指令的方法无法精确定量描述多种共存情绪应如何增减或保持不变。因此需要一种能够以连续分布形式建模情绪状态并在该连续空间中进行转换的编辑框架。

Method: AffectDelta包括以下核心组件：(1) 将情绪编辑建模为八维情绪分布之间的有符号差值转移；(2) 使用冻结的情绪分布预测器（Emotion Distribution Predictor）估计源图像的情绪状态；(3) 源到目标的差值信号编码转移的方向和幅度；(4) 内部转移编码器（transition encoder）与源感知扩散骨干（source-aware diffusion backbone）联合将差值信号解码为上下文相关的场景变化；(5) 构建AffectPair-249K数据集，含248,841个源-目标图像对，配以预测的八维情绪分布，覆盖跨类别与类内转移。

Result: 与六个基线方法的对比实验（包括定量评估和定性比较）表明，AffectDelta在情绪对齐（affective alignment）和内容保留（content preservation）方面均取得更优表现；消融实验进一步验证了各设计选择的有效性。

Conclusion: AffectDelta通过将情绪编辑从离散类别匹配转化为连续八维情绪分布的定量转移，有效解决了现有方法难以处理混合情绪和精确定量情绪变化的问题，在保持图像内容结构的同时实现了更好的情感调控效果。代码和数据集将在论文被接收后公开。

Abstract: Emotion-driven image editing aims to evoke a specified target emotion by modifying emotion-relevant visual cues in a source image, while preserving the overall composition and semantic-structural coherence of the original scene. Existing scene-level editors typically specify the target with a single emotion category and often learn visual transformations from operation-level text instructions. A category collapses a mixed affective endpoint into one dominant label, while language cannot precisely quantify how coexisting emotions should increase, decrease, or remain stable. We introduce AffectDelta, a source-aware editor that treats editing as a transition between eight-dimensional emotion distributions. A frozen Emotion Distribution Predictor estimates the source state, and the signed source-to-target difference encodes the direction and magnitude of the requested transition. Within AffectDelta, an internal transition encoder and a source-aware diffusion backbone jointly translate this signal into context-dependent semantic and appearance changes. To train this formulation, we construct AffectPair-249K, comprising 248,841 source-target pairs with predicted eight-dimensional distributions and spanning both cross-category and within-category transitions. Experiments against six baselines, combining quantitative evaluation with qualitative comparisons, demonstrate improved affective alignment and content preservation, while ablations validate our design choices. Code and dataset will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [96] [From Detection to Localization: A Unified Forensics Framework for Fully Synthetic and Tampered Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02640)
*Annalisa Gallina,Marco Fiorucci,Marco Brigo,Federica Battisti,Lamberto Ballan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的图像取证框架，将生成图像检测从二分类扩展为三分类（真实vs.完全生成vs.篡改），并引入分割分支实现像素级篡改区域定位，在分类精度和IoU分数上优于多个近期基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型的快速发展使得伪造图像越来越逼真，加剧了图像操纵检测的难度。传统方法将图像操纵检测视为二分类任务（真实vs.生成），难以区分和定位不同类型的操纵形式，限制了取证能力。

Method: 在已有检测器的基础上进行扩展，引入统一的多分类框架（真实vs.完全生成vs.篡改三分类），并增加一个分割分支，实现对篡改区域的像素级定位。

Result: 所提出的方法在分类准确率和定位任务的IoU分数上均优于选定的近期基准方法，同时保持了高效的解决方案。

Conclusion: 该多分类加语义分割的统一框架能够同时完成图像真实性分类和篡改区域定位，为多媒体取证提供了更全面有效的解决方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of generative models has significantly worsened the problem of manipulated image detection, as these methods are capable of producing highly realistic forgeries, reinforcing the importance of multimedia forensics. Conventional approaches typically frame image manipulation detection as a binary classification task (real vs. generated), which limits the capability to distinguish and localize different forms of manipulation. To address these constraints, this work extends an existing detector by introducing a unified multiclass framework (real vs. fully generated vs. tampered). In addition to classifying image authenticity, the framework incorporates a segmentation branch to enable pixel-level localization of tampered regions. The proposed approach outperforms selected recent benchmarks, offering an efficient solution with improved classification accuracy and higher IoU scores for the localization task. Find the code at https://github.com/anngal01/From-Detection-to-Localization-A-Unified-Forensics-Framework-for-Fully-Synthetic-and-Tampered-Images.

</details>


### [97] [Learning to Attract and Repel: Dual Quality Margin Learning for Face Recognition (DQM-Face)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02644)
*El Ouanas Belabbaci,Bhavesh Wani,Philipp Terhörst*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DQM-Face框架，通过融合传统的幅值质量估计与新引入的基于squeeze-and-excitation语义注意力的语义质量学习，构建增强的吸引-排斥双质量感知边界，从而在人脸识别的非约束环境中同时提升类内紧凑性和类间判别性，在多个基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非约束环境下的人脸识别面临极端多样的变化，现有基于边界的损失方法仅通过特征幅值建模样本质量，但仅依赖幅值建模容易受到与身份无关的噪声干扰，降低学习表征的可靠性和判别力。

Method: 提出DQM-Face框架，将传统的幅值质量估计与基于squeeze-and-excitation语义注意力的语义质量学习统一起来，联合幅值和语义信号构建增强的质量感知边界。通过吸引边界（attraction）增强类内紧凑性，并通过排斥边界（repulsion）公式显式扩大类间间隔。

Result: 多个具有挑战性的基准数据集上的一致实验表明DQM-Face在性能上持续超越最先进的人脸识别方法。此外，学习到的质量信号在实现识别目标上与人脸图像质量评估高度一致。

Conclusion: 将语义质量建模与吸引-排斥双边界优化统一集成，能够产生更有结构性和判别力的特征几何；学习到的质量信号与识别目标内在对齐，可用于人脸图像质量评估。

Abstract: Face recognition in unconstrained environments remains highly challenging due to diverse and extreme variations encountered in real-world scenarios. To mitigate these effects, existing margin-based approaches model sample quality through feature magnitude. However, magnitude-based modeling alone is susceptible to identity-agnostic noise, which can degrade the reliability and discriminative power of learned representations. In this paper, we propose Dual Quality Margin Learning for Face Recognition (DQM-Face), a novel framework that enables refined attraction and repulsion dynamics during representation learning. Our approach unifies conventional magnitude-based quality estimation with a newly introduced semantic quality learning mechanism, realized via squeeze-and-excitation semantic attention. By jointly leveraging magnitude and semantic cues, we construct enhanced quality-aware margins that adaptively strengthen intra-class compactness through improved attraction during learning. To further enhance inter-class discrimination, we introduce a repulsion margin formulation that explicitly enlarges inter-class separation. The unified integration of semantic quality modeling with dual attraction-repulsion margin optimization results in a more structured and discriminative feature geometry. Extensive experiments on multiple challenging benchmarks demonstrate that DQM-Face consistently outperforms state-of-the-art face recognition methods. Moreover, we show that the quality learned for margin optimization is highly effective for face image quality assessment within the proposed framework, demonstrating that the learned quality signal is intrinsically aligned with the recognition objective. The code is publicly available: https://github.com/RAIB-group/DQM-Face

</details>


### [98] [Physics-Driven Independent Pair Generation for Iterative Self-Supervised Low-Dose CT Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02654)
*Xianlei Han,Shaoyu Wang,Jiancheng Fang,Weiwen Wu,Qiegen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种物理驱动的跨域迭代自监督低剂量CT去噪框架。该方法利用学习到的正弦图先验和LDCT噪声模型对光子计数进行后验推断，分离泊松与高斯噪声分量；随后通过二项式稀疏化与高斯数据稀疏化构建具有近似独立噪声的孪生训练对；最后利用图像域网络训练并前向投影更新先验，跨域迭代逐步细化先验与训练对。实验在模拟与真实LDCT数据上均优于自监督基线，性能接近监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低剂量CT测量包含混合泊松-高斯噪声，但现有自监督去噪方法大多依赖通用图像统计特征，未显式建模这种物理噪声模型，从而限制了其在真实LDCT噪声下的去噪能力。作者旨在通过显式建模噪声物理过程来突破这一局限。

Method: 框架包含三步：(1) 学习到的正弦图先验结合LDCT噪声模型进行光子计数的后验推断，分离泊松分量与高斯分量；(2) 分别对泊松分量进行二项式稀疏化（binomial thinning）、对高斯分量进行高斯数据稀疏化（Gaussian thinning）构建两个分支，并通过残差缩放使各分支噪声水平与观测匹配，从而由单幅低剂量测量生成具有近似独立噪声的训练对；(3) 用该训练对训练图像域网络，其前向投影输出用于更新正弦图先验。通过跨域（正弦域与图像域）迭代，先验与训练对被逐步细化，同时保持与CT采集物理过程的一致性。

Result: 在AAPM、LIDC-IDRI、LoDoPaB-CT模拟数据及真实LDCT数据上的实验表明，该方法在不同剂量水平下均优于所评估的自监督基线方法，且性能与所评估的监督基线方法相当。

Conclusion: 通过将CT物理噪声模型显式引入自监督学习框架并结合跨域迭代策略，该方法能够有效提升低剂量CT去噪性能，在无需配对干净数据的情况下达到接近监督方法的水平，展示了物理先验在自监督医学影像去噪中的潜力。

Abstract: Low-dose computed tomography (LDCT) measurements contain mixed Poisson-Gaussian noise. However, most self-supervised methods rely on generic image statistics and do not explicitly model this noise, which may limit their ability to effectively suppress realistic LDCT noise. To address this issue, we propose a physics-driven framework with cross-domain iteration for self-supervised LDCT denoising. The proposed framework proceeds in three main steps. First, a learned sinogram prior and the LDCT noise model guide posterior inference of photon counts, enabling separation of the Poisson and Gaussian components. Second, the separated Poisson and Gaussian components are respectively processed by binomial thinning and Gaussian data thinning to construct two branches, and residual scaling matches each branch's noise level to that of the observation, yielding a training pair with approximately independent noise realizations from one low-dose measurement. Finally, the pair is used to train an image-domain network whose forward-projected outputs update the prior. Through cross-domain iteration, the prior and the training pair are progressively refined while maintaining consistency with CT acquisition physics. Experiments on simulated data from AAPM, LIDC-IDRI, and LoDoPaB-CT and on real LDCT data show consistent gains over the evaluated self-supervised baselines across dose levels, with performance comparable to the evaluated supervised baseline.

</details>


### [99] [Characterizing Text Branch Sensitivity in Medical Vision-Language Segmentation via Evidence Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02663)
*Ziquan Liu,Zhewei Zhu,Xuyang Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统探究了文本信息在多模态医学图像分割中的实际贡献，通过提出基于证据解耦解码器（EDD）的分析工具，揭示了不同数据集上文本敏感度的显著差异，并发现文本主要通过全局语义调制而非空间定位影响预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管预训练视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在医学图像分割中结合临床文本已展现良好性能，但文本信息对像素级预测的实际贡献程度尚不明确，需要对多模态交互机制进行深入理解。

Method: 首先系统分析多种常用融合策略，发现分割性能对融合模块的选择不敏感；随后提出基于证据深度学习与深度监督的Evidence Decoupling Decoder（EDD），将图像证据与文本调制证据在整个解码过程中解耦，作为内部表征分析工具，同时保持有竞争力的分割性能。

Result: 实验表明文本扰动敏感性在不同数据集间差异显著：在BUSI和BTMRI上删除文本导致性能大幅下降，说明模型强烈依赖文本输入；而在ISIC和Kvasir-SEG上文本影响相对较小。进一步发现文本主要通过全局语义调制影响预测而非独立空间定位，且驱动文本敏感性的具体语义成分因数据集而异。

Conclusion: 这些发现加深了对多模态医学图像分割中模态交互的理解，并为未来模型设计提供了实用洞见，提示不应盲目依赖文本信息，需根据数据集特性合理设计多模态模型。

Abstract: Pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) have shown promising performance in medical image segmentation by incorporating clinical text. However, it remains unclear how much textual information actually contributes to pixel-level predictions. In this work, we systematically investigate the role of text in multimodal medical image segmentation. We first analyze several commonly used fusion strategies and find that segmentation performance is largely insensitive to the choice of fusion module. To further understand modality interactions, we propose an Evidence Decoupling Decoder (EDD) based on evidential deep learning and deep supervision. EDD serves as an internal representation analysis tool that decomposes image evidence and text-modulated evidence throughout the decoding process while maintaining competitive segmentation performance. Experimental results show that the sensitivity to text perturbation varies substantially across datasets. On BUSI and BTMRI, removing text causes catastrophic performance drops, indicating strong model reliance on textual input. On ISIC and Kvasir-SEG, text exerts relatively marginal influence. We further find that text affects predictions mainly through global semantic modulation rather than independent spatial localization, and that the specific semantic components driving text sensitivity differ across datasets. These findings provide a deeper understanding of modality interaction in multimodal medical image segmentation and offer practical insights for future model design.

</details>


### [100] [Query Rewriting for Complex Object Segmentation in 4D Gaussian Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02664)
*Thanh-Khoi Nguyen,Thien-Phuc Tran,Minh-Triet Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的查询重写策略，将冗长的描述性查询转化为简洁的关键词形式，以提升4D高斯表示中语言引导的复杂目标分割性能，在HyperNeRF和Neu3D数据集上显著提升时序定位精度（60.92%→92.21%）和空间分割指标（vIoU 20.08%→76.94%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D高斯表示框架虽在语言引导的动态场景理解中表现良好，但对包含噪声上下文信息的冗长叙述式查询高度敏感，导致复杂目标分割性能不稳定。

Method: 受检索增强语言模型和关键词引导查询重写研究的启发，提出一种无需训练的重新解释（reinterpretation）策略，将长描述性查询转换为简洁的关键词形式，逐步降低语言噪声，同时保留与目标中心表示相关的语义锚点。

Result: 在HyperNeRF和Neu3D数据集上的实验表明，简洁重写查询显著提升时序定位和空间分割性能，平均时序准确率从60.92%提升至92.21%，平均vIoU从20.08%提升至76.94%，且无需额外微调。

Conclusion: 消融实验表明更短、更聚焦关键词的查询能产生稳定的视频特征相似度分布，并与目标中心的高斯表示实现更好的对齐，验证了查询精简对于4D高斯场景理解的有效性。

Abstract: Recent 4D Gaussian representation frameworks have demonstrated strong performance in language-guided dynamic scene understanding. However, these methods remain highly sensitive to verbose and narrative-style queries that contain noisy contextual information. In this paper, we investigate the impact of query rewriting for complex object segmentation in 4D Gaussian representations. Inspired by recent findings in retrieval-augmented language models and keyword-guided query reformulation, we propose a training-free reinterpretation strategy that transforms long descriptive queries into concise keyword-grounded forms. Our approach progressively reduces linguistic noise while preserving semantic anchors relevant to object-centric representations. Experiments on HyperNeRF and Neu3D demonstrate that concise rewritten queries significantly improve both temporal localization and spatial segmentation performance. In particular, our method improves average temporal accuracy from 60.92% to 92.21% and average vIoU from 20.08% to 76.94% without any additional fine-tuning. Extensive ablation studies further reveal that shorter, keyword-focused queries consistently yield stable video-feature similarity distributions and better alignment with object-centric Gaussian representations

</details>


### [101] [Genesis: A Generative Engine for Hierarchical Satellite Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02683)
*Subash Khanal,Yangzhi Cui,Daniel Cher,Eric Xing,Brian Wei,Srikumar Sastry,Nathan Jacobs*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个新的任务——多尺度瓦片补全（multi-scale tile completion），并开发了Genesis生成引擎，通过结合垂直方向超分辨率模型与水平方向掩码外推模型，在四叉树结构上同时生成跨尺度和跨空间一致的卫星影像金字塔。作者还构建了dense500数据集和金字塔级评估指标来验证方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测天然具有多尺度特性，地理空间任务跨越不同分辨率，卫星影像被组织为层叠的金字塔瓦片结构。然而现有的卫星影像生成模型只沿单一方向操作：要么放大提升单个瓦片分辨率，要么在固定尺度下延展影像范围。没有任何现有方法能生成在尺度和空间上同时保持一致性的完整金字塔，即高缩放瓦片必须与其细化的粗尺度上下文及相邻瓦片一致。这一关键空白促使作者提出新任务与方法。

Method: 提出了Genesis生成引擎，通过四叉树结构上组合两种专门算子来同时协调尺度和空间两个轴：一个垂直方向的超分辨率模型用于跨缩放级别的一致性细化，一个水平方向的基于掩膜的外推模型用于相邻瓦片之间的无缝衔接。该引擎能将任意初始配置下的稀疏种子瓦片传播为无缝的多分辨率地图。此外构建了dense500数据集（覆盖多种地理区域的完全观测多尺度金字塔数据集）以及一套金字塔级评估指标。

Result: 每个子算子在其子任务上都达到了最先进的性能，Genesis引擎能够从任意初始配置将稀疏种子传播为无缝、多分辨率的完整地图。

Conclusion: Genesis首次实现了跨尺度和跨空间同时一致性的多尺度卫星影像金字塔生成，填补了现有方法只能单轴操作的空白，并提供了数据集与指标以支撑该新任务的研究。

Abstract: Earth observation is fundamentally multi-scale; geospatial tasks span varied resolutions, and satellite imagery is organized into cascading tile pyramids that nest fine detail within wide coverage. Current generative models of satellite imagery, however, operate along a single axis: they either zoom to enhance a single tile's resolution or pan to extend imagery at a fixed scale. As a result, no existing method produces a complete pyramid that stays consistent across both scale and space, where a high-zoom tile must agree with the coarse context it refines and with the neighbors it meets. Motivated by this gap, we introduce a new task, multi-scale tile completion: given a sparse set of seed tiles at arbitrary zoom levels and positions, synthesize a complete, uniform quadtree that is globally consistent across both scale and space. We approach this task with Genesis, a generative engine that brings both axes together by composing two specialized operators over the quadtree: a vertical super-resolution model and a horizontal mask-based outpainting model, producing pyramids that are consistent across zoom levels and seamless across neighboring tiles. Each operator achieves state-of-the-art results on its subtask, and the engine propagates sparse seeds into seamless, multi-resolution maps from any initial configuration. To evaluate the task and benchmark Genesis, we introduce dense500, a fully observed multi-scale pyramid dataset spanning diverse geographic regions, together with a suite of pyramid-level metrics. Code, models, and our dataset are available at https://github.com/mvrl/genesis.

</details>


### [102] [GaLe: memory-efficient Global Approximate and Local Exact features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02689)
*Alberto Ancilotto,Elisabetta Farella*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GaLe是一种内存高效的技术，无需重新训练即可将预训练网络部署到资源受限的嵌入式设备上。它通过将特征图划分为局部精确表示和全局近似表示两部分，在保持精确推理性能的同时实现了高达65%的加速和90%的RAM减少（在Cortex-M33上），并支持混合CNN-Transformer模型中的全局操作与注意力机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式设备缺乏GPU机器的资源，现有推理方法存在两难困境：基于分块的推理方法计算开销高，基于近似的推理方法导致精度损失。因此需要一种既能保持推理精度又能高效部署到受限设备的解决方案。

Method: GaLe将特征图拆分为两个组成部分：局部精确（Le）表示用于保留细粒度细节，全局近似（Ga）表示用于保留长距离依赖关系。与标准分块（tiling）不同，GaLe能够支持混合CNN-Transformer模型中的全局操作和注意力机制。

Result: 在ImageNet数据集上验证，GaLe匹配精确推理的性能，同时在Cortex-M33上相比基于分块的推理实现了高达65%的加速和90%的RAM减少。此外，GaLe在分类、检测和生成任务中展示了其通用性。

Conclusion: GaLe展示了其作为资源高效架构设计基础（foundation）的潜力，能够在无需重新训练的情况下实现预训练网络的边缘端部署，兼顾精度、速度和内存效率。

Abstract: Embedded devices typically lack the resources of GPU-equipped machines, and existing inference methods suffer from either high computational overhead (patch-based) or accuracy loss (approximation-based). We propose GaLe, a memory-efficient technique that enables the deployment of pretrained networks on constrained devices without retraining. GaLe partitions feature maps into two components: a local exact (Le) representation that preserves fine details and a global approximate (Ga) representation that retains long-range dependencies. Unlike standard tiling, GaLe supports global operations and attention mechanisms found in hybrid CNN-transformer models. Validated on ImageNet, our method matches exact-inference performance while achieving up to 65% speedup and 90% RAM reduction on a Cortex-M33 compared to patch-based inference. We further demonstrate GaLe's versatility across classification, detection, and generation tasks, highlighting its potential as a foundation for resource-efficient architecture design.

</details>


### [103] [Generating Medical Image Counterfactuals using Causal Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02697)
*David A. Kelly,Tom Yaacov,Nathan Blake,Sander Beckers,Hana Chockler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需生成模型的医学图像反事实生成框架，直接从分类器的因果证据中构建反事实图像，实现确定性、无需额外训练的、可在用户指定区域内可控编辑的解释方法，且在真实医学影像数据集上优于生成式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在医学图像诊断中表现优异，但临床部署受限于可解释性不足。现有反事实图像解释方法依赖生成对抗网络或扩散模型等辅助生成模型，用另一个黑盒模型解释分类器，难以将分类器的决策过程与生成器的归纳偏置分离开来。

Method: 提出一种无需生成模型的反事实生成框架：直接从分类器内部提取的因果证据构建反事实图像。该方法具有确定性、无需额外模型训练，并支持在用户指定的感兴趣区域内进行可控编辑。

Result: 在真实医学影像数据集上的实验表明，该方法能够成功改变分类器的预测结果，同时与原始图像的差异比生成式基线方法更小，为分类器决策边界提供了更直接、透明的视角。

Conclusion: 通过摒弃生成模型的依赖，直接从分类器本身提取因果证据来构建反事实图像，提供了一种更直接、透明且高效的解释途径，加深了对分类器决策边界的理解。

Abstract: Deep learning models have achieved impressive performance in medical image diagnosis, yet their deployment in clinical settings remains constrained by limited explainability. Counterfactual images provide one means of auditing model behavior by showing how an image would need to change for a classifier to produce a different prediction. Existing approaches typically generate such explanations using auxiliary models, including generative adversarial networks and diffusion models. While often capable of producing visually realistic images, these methods explain one black-box model using another, making it difficult to separate the classifier's decision-making process from the inductive biases of the generator.
  We propose a novel counterfactual-generation framework that requires no generative model. Instead, counterfactuals are constructed directly from causal evidence extracted from the classifier. The resulting approach is deterministic, requires no additional model training, and enables controllable edits within user-specified regions of interest. Experiments on real-world medical imaging datasets demonstrate that the proposed method successfully changes classifier predictions while remaining closer to the original image than generative baselines, providing a more direct and transparent view of the classifier's decision boundary.

</details>


### [104] [A Top-Down Framework for Metric-Scale Athlete Localization from Single Broadcast Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02705)
*Thanh-Khoi Nguyen,Hoang-Phuc Nguyen,Linh-Huynh,Minh-Triet Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于单帧广播画面的自上而下运动员度量级世界坐标定位框架。核心贡献包括：边界感知自适应切片（Boundary-Aware Adaptive Tiling），一种面向语义的扩展切片推理方法，通过迭代扩展切片边界保证目标完整包含，缓解极端尺度差异下的边界切分伪影与召回率下降；以及将RTMPose-X改造为双关键点估计器（骨盆与地面投影点），并通过相机标定的光线投射将2D地面投影确定性提升至世界坐标。在公开测试集上达到97.44的LocSim分数与0.9128的mAP，较基线提升超21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单帧广播画面中的运动员世界坐标定位极具挑战性，主要体现在超高分辨率图像中极端尺度差异带来的目标边界切分、召回率退化等问题，这限制了对透视畸变等核心误差源的隔离与量化，缺乏高精度的度量级定位方案。

Method: 提出自上而下框架，包含三大技术：(1) 边界感知自适应切片，在标准切片推理基础上利用粗粒度边界框预测迭代扩展切片边界，确保目标完整包含且无需架构改动；(2) 将RTMPose-X适配为双关键点估计器（骨盆和地面投影点），采用为几何耦合点对优化的Gated Attention Unit改进结构；(3) 通过相机标定光线投射，将2D地面投影点确定性提升为世界坐标。

Result: 在公开测试集上实现97.44的LocSim分数和0.9128的mAP，超越基线超过21%，验证了该方法在高分辨率尺度变化场景下的有效性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 边界感知自适应切片有效解决了极端尺度差异下的边界切分问题，使透视畸变成为可隔离的主要误差源；结合双关键点估计与标定光线投射，建立了面向高分辨率尺度变化场景的鲁棒运动员世界坐标定位解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate world-coordinate localization of athletes from single-frame broadcast footage is inherently challenging due to extreme scale disparities in ultra-high-resolution imagery. In this paper, we propose a top-down framework for metric-scale athlete localization from a single calibrated frame. Our approach centers on three key contributions. First, we propose Boundary-Aware Adaptive Tiling, a semantics-guided extension of standard sliced inference. By iteratively expanding tile boundaries based on coarse bounding-box predictions, it systematically ensures full object containment, effectively mitigating boundary-splitting artifacts through a lightweight pipeline adaptation without architectural modifications. By substantially mitigating recall degradation under extreme scale variance, Boundary-Aware Adaptive Tiling enables us to isolate perspective distortion as the primary source of residual localization error. Second, we adapt the RTMPose-X architecture into a specialized two-keypoint estimator (pelvis and ground projection), employing a reformulated Gated Attention Unit optimized for this geometrically coupled point pair, and then deterministically lift the 2D ground projections into world coordinates via camera-calibrated ray casting. On the public test set, our method achieves a LocSim score of 97.44 and an mAP of 0.9128, outperforming the baseline by over 21 \% and establishing a robust solution for high-resolution scale variance.

</details>


### [105] [MV-dVRK: A Multi-Viewpoint Benchmark for Spatial Surgical Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02717)
*Guido Caccianiga,Sergey Prokudin,Yutong Chen,Bernard Javot,Rachael L'Orsa,Omer Burak Aladağ,Yarden Sharon,Jens Rolinger,Ivan Capobianco,Anton Deguet,Siyu Tang,Katherine J. Kuchenbecker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了MV-dVRK，这是首个结合多曝光同步立体视点与精确表面几何及相机位姿的离体手术数据集。利用该数据集，作者系统比较了稀疏视角下多种3D重建方法，发现两内窥镜时多立体重建覆盖率最高，三视角时基于优化的多视角方法最佳（1mm容差内覆盖67%真实表面点），优于前馈基础模型（43%）。数据集还包含十个动态序列供未来多视角手术感知研究使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏多视角3D重建在大规模训练和优化技术下取得了显著进展，但其在真实内窥镜图像上的效果从未被严格评估过。当前临床遥操作机器人仅配备单一立体相机，多视角数据极为稀缺，阻碍了多视角手术感知方法的发展与验证。

Method: 构建MV-dVRK离体手术数据集：静态子集提供密集SfM参考几何（经工业3D扫描仪验证）、真实相机位姿和稀疏视角测试集；动态子集包含十个涵盖多种手术任务、运动学复杂度递增且伴随组织变形的序列。利用该数据集系统比较零样本单目、立体、多立体及多视角重建方法在不同视点数量下的表现。

Result: 双内窥镜设置下多立体重建覆盖率最高；三视角设置下基于优化的多视角方法表现最优，在1mm容差内覆盖67%真实表面点并恢复高精度相对相机位姿；相比之下前馈基础模型仅覆盖43%真实表面。数据集同时提供动态序列以支持未来研究。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在稀疏多视角手术重建中，基于优化的传统方法在几何精度上仍优于前馈基础模型；MV-dVRK数据集为多视角手术感知提供了首个标准化基准，填补了该领域对真实内窥镜图像评估的空白。

Abstract: Large-scale training and refined optimization techniques have greatly improved sparse multi-view 3D reconstruction. Despite their relevance to surgery, such methods have never before been rigorously evaluated on real endoscopic images. Current clinical telerobots deploy a single stereo camera inside the patient, making multi-viewpoint data extremely rare. This paper presents MV-dVRK, the first ex-vivo surgical dataset to combine multiple exposure-synchronized stereo viewpoints with accurate surface geometry and camera poses. The static subset of the benchmark provides dense SfM reference geometry, validated against an industrial 3D scanner, together with ground-truth camera poses and sparse-view test sets. We use MV-dVRK to systematically compare zero-shot monocular, stereo, multi-stereo, and multi-view 3D reconstruction methods as the number of viewpoints increases. With two endoscopes, multi-stereo reconstruction achieves the highest coverage. With a third viewpoint, optimization-based multi-view methods perform best, covering 67% of ground-truth surface points within a 1 mm tolerance and recovering highly accurate relative camera poses. By contrast, feed-forward foundation models cover only 43% of the ground-truth surface in the same setting. MV-dVRK also includes ten dynamic sequences spanning multiple surgical tasks, with increasing kinematic complexity and tissue deformation, providing a basis for future research in multi-viewpoint surgical perception. The project is available at: https://mv-dvrk.is.mpg.de.

</details>


### [106] [InceptionGS: Generative Bootstrapping for Large-Scale Gaussian Splatting under Unstructured View Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02747)
*Tianheng Lu,Guangyu Wang,Ruqi Huang,Lu Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出InceptionGS，一种通过平衡重建与生成来解决大规模场景非结构化采样/视角稀疏问题的Gaussian splatting引导方法。该方法从初始Gaussian splatting出发，软性融入场景与视角自适应的生成先验，在修复因视角缺失导致的问题区域的同时保持其他区域质量，实验证明其在真实大规模场景上的优越性与广泛适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模场景数字化需要在所有视角下保持一致的视觉渲染，但收集覆盖每个细节的多视角图像因场景复杂、采集成本、疏忽或可达性限制而不可行，导致采样视角高度非结构化——大部分场景覆盖良好但某些区域观测不足。现有基于重建的方法对视角稀疏敏感，而基于生成的方法在泛化性、可控性和3D一致性方面存在问题。

Method: InceptionGS从初始Gaussian splatting出发，通过软性引入场景与视角自适应的生成先验，合理地重新审视并修复因视角稀疏导致的问题区域，同时保留其他区域的高质量渲染，从而实现重建与生成的微妙平衡。

Result: 在真实世界大规模场景上的广泛实验证明了InceptionGS在处理非结构化图像和提升高保真Gaussian splatting方面的优越性与广泛适用性。

Conclusion: InceptionGS为大规模场景中视角稀疏的非结构化图像采集提供了一种有效的解决方案，通过融合重建与生成策略实现了兼顾修复能力与质量控制的高保真渲染。

Abstract: Achieving truly immersive large-scale scene digitization necessitates consistent and visually pleasing rendering across all possible viewing perspectives. However, collecting multi-view images covering every fine detail of a large-scale scene is prohibitive due to scene complexity, capture cost, negligence, or accessibility constraints. As a result, the sampled views tend to be highly unstructured -- the majority of the scene is well covered yet certain regions inevitably lack sufficient observations. Existing reconstruction based methods are vulnerable to view scarcity while generation based approaches suffer from generalization, controllability, and 3D consistency issues. To address this challenge, we propose InceptionGS, which bootstraps Gaussian splatting by subtly balancing reconstruction and generation. Starting from an initial Gaussian splatting, InceptionGS reasonably rethinks and repairs problematic regions caused by view scarcity while preserving the quality elsewhere, by softly incorporating scene- and view-adaptive generative priors. Extensive experiments on real-world large-scale scenes demonstrate the superiority and broad applicability of our approach in handling unstructured imagery and boosting high-fidelity Gaussian splatting. Please refer to the supplementary video for better visual demonstrations.

</details>


### [107] [Balancing Frequencies and Pixels in Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02748)
*Lucas Degeorge,Paul Couairon,Arijit Ghosh,Alexei A. Efros,David Picard,Vicky Kalogeiton*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种频谱平衡的损失函数（f-loss），结合频率域与像素域监督，通过先强调频率学习后过渡到像素空间精化的训练策略，解决像素空间流模型训练中低频偏置导致的高频细节学习缓慢问题，可加速收敛至40%并提升FID和感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然图像遵循1/f^2谱分布，像素空间重建目标对所有空间误差统一处理，导致低频域主导优化信号并延迟纹理和边缘等感知关键高频细节的学习。作者将此目标层面的频谱失衡识别为训练像素空间流模型的关键低效所在。

Method: 提出Focal Log-Frequency Loss (f-loss)——一种跨频率均衡学习信号的频谱平衡目标。在此基础上设计简单训练策略：早期强调频域学习以捕获所有频率成分，随后过渡到标准像素空间v-loss进行空间精化。该方法无需架构改动，可直接替代流匹配损失。

Result: 在多种模型规模下，该方法可将收敛速度提升高达40%，并持续改善FID和感知保真度指标。

Conclusion: 该方法是简明的、即插即用的流匹配损失替代方案，通过频率与像素监督的时序结合有效缓解像素损失低频偏置，使训练信号与模型演化需求对齐。

Abstract: Natural images follow a $1/f^2$ spectral distribution: most signal energy lies in the low spatial frequencies, while the perceptually important structures such as textures and edges occupy sparse high-frequency bands. Pixel-space reconstruction objectives, however, treat all spatial errors uniformly, causing low frequencies to dominate the optimization signal and delaying the learning of fine-scale details. In this work, we identify this objective-level spectral imbalance as a key inefficiency in training pixel-space flow models. To address it, we propose a Focal Log-Frequency Loss (f-loss), a spectrally balanced objective that equalizes the learning signal across frequencies, emphasizing high-frequency components that are otherwise underrepresented in pixel-space objectives. Building on this, we introduce a simple training strategy that combines frequency and pixel supervision: we first emphasize frequency-domain learning early to capture all frequencies, and then transition to standard pixel-space v-loss for spatial refinement. This balancing mitigates the low-frequency bias of pixel losses and aligns the training signal with the evolving needs of the model. Our approach is conceptually simple, requires no architectural changes, and acts as a drop-in replacement for flow matching losses. Across multiple model scales, it accelerates convergence by up to 40% while consistently improving FID and perceptual fidelity. We will release code and models.

</details>


### [108] [Multi-Tool Image Editing Attribution in Facial Forgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02751)
*Sheng Liu,Qiang Sheng,Danding Wang,Yu Li,Chenming Zhou,Juan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次提出多工具图像编辑归因（MIEA）任务，旨在从经多种编辑工具处理的人脸图像中识别所有涉及的编辑工具。作者构建了包含50万+张编辑人脸的MultiEdit数据集（覆盖换脸和多种面部增强六类工具），并设计了DPEC方法，结合空间域与频率域的局部编辑痕迹捕捉及基于误差的课程学习策略，在多步编辑场景下优于九种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑归因方法基于单一工具假设，只能识别一种编辑工具，但现实中用户常使用多工具组合编辑，不同工具留下的痕迹相互叠加混杂，现有方法难以应对。

Method: 首先构建MultiEdit数据集（50万+张人脸图，覆盖Deepfake换脸及多种面部增强共六类工具）以模拟真实多步编辑操作；通过数据分析启发，设计DPEC方法——从空间域和频率域捕获可区分、具有局部感知的编辑工具痕迹，并采用基于误差的课程学习策略训练。

Result: DPEC在最多五步编辑的人脸图像上，优于九种对比方法。

Conclusion: 本文填补了多工具编辑归因空白，提出的数据集与方法为多工具编辑场景下的溯源提供了有效解决方案，实验验证了其在复合编辑痕迹场景下的优越性能。

Abstract: As generative AI tools become increasingly powerful and easy to use, people can easily edit portrait images with a prompt, necessitating the task of image editing attribution, which predicts the involved editing tools from the given image. Existing attribution methods hold the single-tool assumption and can only attribute a specific editing tool, but struggle to handle the more complex and increasingly common multi-tool editing scenarios, where artifacts left by different editing tools are composite and overlapped. To address this gap, we explore Multi-Tool Image Editing Attribution (MIEA), which aims to identify multiple editing tools involved in a multi-tool edited facial image. To simulate the real-life editing operations on facial images, we then construct a new dataset, MultiEdit, which contains 500k+ edited facial images and covers six types of editing tools that support face swapping (Deepfake) and various facial enhancements. Inspired by the findings from data analysis, we design DPEC, a multi-tool attribution method that can capture distinguishable, locality-aware editing tool traces from both spatial and frequency domains with the support of an error-based curriculum learning strategy. Experiments show \Method\ outperforms nine methods for facial images edited in at most five steps.

</details>


### [109] [Video-Based Palm-Vein Authentication under Challenging Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02776)
*Xiaofeng Yan,Kechen Liu,Abhilash Venkatesh,Cathy Zhang,Xia Zhou,Salvatore Stolfo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了首个公开的视频级掌静脉数据集CUP（哥伦比亚大学掌静脉数据集），覆盖干净、温热、潮湿和脏污四种表面条件。系统性评估了21种识别器后发现脏污条件下识别精度大幅下降（平均EER约翻四倍）。作者提出了一种无额外训练参数的两轴稳健化方案：时间轴上对多帧做共识聚合消除瞬态噪声，空间轴上使用显著性引导的区域级最优传输匹配，绕过损坏区域。该方法在CUP所有表面状态下在EER、TAR@FAR=0.01和Rank-1指标上均最优，仅需4.3M参数和3.1 GFLOPs；挂接在四种冻结骨干网上可降低29-37%平均EER，在四个公开单图数据集上区域匹配依然有效。初步审计发现温和条件下存在身体水分和性别相关的公平性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实部署中的掌静脉识别系统会遭遇到汗液、污垢、光照变化、运动以及温度导致的血管可见性变化等表面噪声退化，但这些鲁棒性问题因缺乏在真实条件下采集的数据而研究不足。作者旨在填补这一空白，推动掌静脉识别在非理想采集条件下的鲁棒性和公平性研究。

Method: （1）构建CUP数据集：首个公开的视频级掌静脉数据集，每个手掌在四种表面条件（干净基线、温热、潮湿、脏污）下记录，并配对人体生理与人口统计学元数据；（2）基准评估21种识别器，涵盖静态、视频和多帧聚合架构；（3）提出双轴鲁棒性方案：时间上利用传感器已返回的多帧进行一致性共识聚合以抵消瞬时污染；空间上提出无学习参数的自适应匹配器，融合全局余弦相似度与显著性引导的区域级最优传输匹配，绕过被污染的区域；（4）贴附四种冻结SOTA骨干网评估迁移效果，并在四个公开单图像数据集上进行泛化验证。

Result: 在干净手掌上表现可靠的模型在脏污条件下精度大幅下降，平均EER约翻四倍。所提双轴方案在CUP全部四种表面条件下均取得最优EER、TAR@FAR=0.01和Rank-1成绩，模型仅4.3M参数和3.1 GFLOPs，远低于视频模型的成本。在四种冻结SOTA骨干网上无需重新训练即可将其平均EER降低29-37%；在四个公开单图像数据集中区域匹配本身仍能改善性能。公平性审计在十项人口学与生理特征中发现两个温热条件下的差距（身体水分与性别），且通过多重比较校正后依然显著。

Conclusion: 掌静脉识别在实际部署中对表面污染（尤其是脏污）极度脆弱，但可以通过免训练的多帧时间聚合与显著性引导的区域级最优传输匹配实现高性价比的鲁棒性恢复。CUP数据集为掌静脉识别在挑战性非理想条件下的研究提供了标准化基准，同时揭示了传统评测长期忽视的环境扰动对识别和公平性的影响。数据集将公开发布以促进社区研究。

Abstract: Palm-vein biometrics are increasingly used for secure, contactless authentication. Yet real-world deployment exposes them to surface noise (sweat, dirt), illumination and motion variation, and temperature-driven changes in vascular visibility, which remain underexplored for lack of data captured under such conditions. To study these effects, we introduce the Columbia University Palm-vein (CUP) dataset, to our knowledge the first public video-based palm-vein dataset. CUP records every palm under four surface conditions (a clean baseline, warm, wet, and dirty) and pairs each subject with physiological and demographic metadata. On it we benchmark twenty-one recognizers spanning static, video, and multi-frame aggregation architectures. Models that verify reliably on clean palms lose most of their accuracy on dirty ones, and the mean equal error rate (EER) roughly quadruples. We recover much of that robustness along both axes of the capture. Temporally, a consensus over the few frames the sensor already returns cancels transient corruption; spatially, a test-time matcher that adds no learned parameters fuses the global cosine with a saliency-steered region-level optimal transport that routes the comparison around corrupted regions. The full design leads on every surface of CUP in EER, TAR@FAR=0.01, and Rank-1, at 4.3M parameters and 3.1 GFLOPs, a fraction of the video models' cost. Attached to four frozen state-of-the-art backbones it cuts their mean EER by 29-37% without retraining, and on four public single-image datasets the regional matching alone still helps. A preliminary audit across ten demographic and physiological traits finds two warm-condition gaps, along body water and gender, that survive multiple-comparison correction. CUP will be released for non-commercial research use at https://github.com/MobileX-CU/CUP_v1 upon publication.

</details>


### [110] [AutoCompass: Accurate Visual Localization on Public Maps by Learning from Weak Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02798)
*Javier Tirado-Garín,Alan Savio Paul,Shuai Chen,Axel Barroso-Laguna,Tommaso Cavallari,Daniyar Turmukhambetov,Victor Adrian Prisacariu,Eric Brachmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AutoCompass是一种针对神经地图匹配器的监督训练方法，能够从不准确的绝对位姿标签（如GPS）中学习，通过消除对航向标签的依赖、利用GPS容差区域以及引入SLAM/SfM相对位姿来提升训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经地图匹配器通常依赖大规模地理参考图像数据集进行训练，但这些数据集中包含的位置和航向标签往往带有噪声，从而影响训练模型的质量。

Method: 提出AutoCompass监督方法，包含三个关键策略：(1) 论证航向标签是不必要的，仅使用原始GPS标签训练，模型可自动预测准确的航向；(2) 在原始GPS周围定义容差区域以提升位置精度；(3) 利用SLAM或SfM获得的训练图像间的相对位姿作为更精确的训练信号。

Result: 在驾驶和以自我为中心（egocentric）的多个基准测试中，AutoCompass一致性地优于传统依赖绝对位姿标签训练的模型。

Conclusion: 相较于对绝对位姿标签的强依赖，AutoCompass通过更灵活的监督方式有效应对标签噪声问题，提升了神经地图匹配器的训练效果和泛化能力。

Abstract: Neural map matchers estimate an image's 3-DoF pose relative to a 2D map. These models are trained on large-scale datasets of geo-referenced images, whose position and heading labels often contain noise that affects the trained models. To address this, we present AutoCompass, a supervision approach for training neural map matchers from inaccurate absolute pose labels. First, we show that heading labels are unnecessary: trained from raw GPS labels, models learn to predict accurate headings, automatically. Second, defining a tolerance region around raw GPS improves positional accuracy. Third, if available, our supervision uses relative poses between training images, obtained via SLAM or SfM, which provide a more accurate training signal. Across driving and egocentric benchmarks, AutoCompass consistently outperforms counterparts trained with the usual strong reliance on absolute pose labels.

</details>


### [111] [GDB-Reward: From Evaluation Metrics to Training Rewards for Graphic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02813)
*Adrienne Deganutti,Purvanshi Mehta,Simon Hadfield,Andrew Gilbert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GDB-Reward框架，将异构图形设计评估指标统一转化为强化学习奖励，从而在不微调冻结的文本到图像生成模型的前提下，通过提示优化提升图形设计质量（排版、布局、颜色、视觉传达等多维约束满足度）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像模型在自然图像合成上表现出色，但在图形设计领域表现不佳，因为该领域依赖于排版、布局、颜色及视觉沟通等精确约束的满足。提示优化是扩散模型微调的廉价替代方案，但需要信息量丰富的奖励函数，而生成过程完全不可微。强化学习只要求标量奖励用于排序候选输出，因此作者提出将现有的设计评估指标本身作为强化学习的奖励信号。

Method: 提出GDB-Reward框架，系统性地将异构的图形设计评估指标转换为统一的强化学习奖励，在保持图像生成器完全冻结的前提下，通过强化学习优化提示词以提升输出对设计规范的符合度。

Result: 实验表明GDB-Reward提供了有效的优化目标，在感知质量、渲染保真度和空间准确性上显著提升了设计方案规范的符合度，同时图像生成器保持完全冻结。

Conclusion: 异构、不可微的评估指标可以从被动基准测试角色转变为强化学习的有效优化目标，特别是在缺乏可微监督的领域；这为图形设计及其他类似领域提供了新的优化范式。

Abstract: Text-to-image models excel at natural image synthesis but struggle with graphic design, where success depends on satisfying precise constraints on typography, layout, color, and visual communication. While prompt optimization offers an attractive alternative to expensive diffusion model fine-tuning, learning prompts for frozen image generators requires informative reward functions despite the entirely non-differentiable generation process. Reinforcement learning does not require differentiable objectives; it requires only scalar rewards capable of ranking candidate outputs. This raises a simple question: can design evaluation metrics themselves become reinforcement learning rewards? Our central contribution is GDB-Reward, a framework that systematically transforms heterogeneous graphic design evaluation metrics into a unified reinforcement learning reward. Experiments demonstrate that GDB-Reward provides an effective optimization objective, substantially improving adherence to the design specification in perceptual quality, rendering fidelity, and spatial accuracy while keeping the image generator entirely frozen. More broadly, our results demonstrate that heterogeneous, non-differentiable evaluation metrics can move beyond passive benchmarking to become effective optimization objectives for reinforcement learning in domains where differentiable supervision is unavailable.

</details>


### [112] [Benchmarking RAW and RGB Restoration in Image Signal Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02831)
*Zihao Lu,Radu Timofte,Marcos V. Conde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基准测试，对比在固定ISP流水线中两种盲图像恢复位置：RAW域恢复（pre-ISP）与sRGB域恢复（post-ISP），涵盖多种手机设备组、学习型ISP、退化类型及代表性恢复模型。结果表明恢复位置本身不决定性能，而恢复模型与成像流水线的对齐程度才是关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代相机通过ISP将RAW传感器测量值转换为sRGB图像。现有盲图像恢复研究对恢复模型应放置在ISP之前（RAW域）还是之后（sRGB域）缺乏系统性的基准评估，导致结论分歧且实验条件不一。

Method: 构建了包含四种智能手机设备组、两种学习型ISP、三种退化类型（噪声、模糊、噪声加模糊）以及多个代表性RAW与RGB恢复模型的基准测试框架。在固定ISP条件下，系统对比pre-ISP（RAW域）与post-ISP（sRGB域）两种恢复放置策略的性能差异。

Result: 发现恢复放置本身并不决定性能；RAW域恢复策略优于最佳通用RGB恢复模型；但对ISP变换感知的RGB恢复模型取得了最佳总体性能。图像重建性能强烈依赖于恢复模型与目标成像流水线的对齐。

Conclusion: 建议将恢复位置（placement）和ISP感知监督能力作为图像恢复实验报告中的关键因素，以推动该领域更严谨的可比性与可复现性研究，同时开源了代码库。

Abstract: Modern cameras transform RAW sensor measurements into sRGB images through an image signal processor (ISP). We benchmark two placements for blind restoration around a fixed ISP: (A) pre-ISP restoration in the RAW domain and (B) post-ISP restoration in the sRGB domain. The benchmark covers four smartphone device groups, two learned ISPs, three degradation regimes--noise, blur, and joint noise and blur--, and several representative RAW and RGB restoration models. Our results show that placement alone does not determine performance. The RAW restoration strategy outperforms the best generic RGB restoration models. However, RGB restoration models trained considering the ISP transformations, achieve the best overall performance. Our novel benchmark demonstrates that the image reconstruction performance strongly depends on the alignment between the restoration model and the target imaging pipeline. We consequently recommend reporting restoration placement and ISP-aware supervision as key experimental factors. Our code is available at https://github.com/mv-lab/AISP

</details>


### [113] [Efficient All-in-One Weather Restoration using Spectral Harmonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02839)
*Paula Garrido-Mellado,Daniel Feijoo,Yuning Cui,Alvaro Garcia,Marcos V. Conde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FReSH-IR，一种轻量级的全合一图像复原方法，通过在分层编码器-解码器架构的每一尺度显式地将特征分解为高频和低频分量，并结合基于傅里叶的跳跃连接进行频谱重建与谱和谐化处理，在雾、雨、雪等多种恶劣天气退化场景下实现与Transformer模型相近的复原质量，同时减少80%的参数和计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶劣天气条件（如雨、雾、雪）会显著降低图像质量，影响人类感知和物理AI系统。现有复原方法计算开销大，难以处理高分辨率图像，且无法有效应对多种不同类型的退化，亟需一种轻量级且高效的全合一复原方法。

Method: 提出频率重建与频谱和谐化（Frequency Reconstruction via Spectral Harmonization, FReSH-IR）方法：在分层编码器-解码器架构的每个尺度上，将特征表示显式分解为高频和低频分量；通过傅里叶跳跃连接将频谱分解与空间处理相结合，以捕捉互补的频率信息而不损失空间细节。

Result: 在多种恶劣天气退化场景下，FReSH-IR达到与基于Transformer的模型相近的复原质量，同时参数和计算量减少80%。大量实验表明该方法在效率与性能之间提供了良好的权衡，特别适用于资源受限系统。

Conclusion: FReSH-IR通过显式的高低频分解与傅里叶跳跃连接实现了高质量、轻量级的全合一图像复原，展现了在资源受限的实际应用中出色的效率-性能平衡，具有良好的实用性前景。

Abstract: Adverse weather conditions such as rain, haze, and snow significantly degrade image quality, posing challenges for both human perception and physical AI. Existing restoration methods require large computational budgets, struggling to process high-resolution images and handle different degradations. In this paper, we present Frequency Reconstruction via Spectral Harmonization, a novel lightweight all-in-one restoration method that explicitly decomposes feature representations into high- and low-frequency components at each scale of a hierarchical encoder-decoder architecture. By combining spectral decomposition with spatial processing through Fourier-based skip connections, FReSH-IR captures complementary frequency information without sacrificing spatial detail. Our approach achieves similar restoration quality with 80% fewer parameters and operations than transformer-based models. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method offers a great efficiency-performance trade-off, highlighting its practical applications in constrained-resource systems.

</details>


### [114] [RoGe: Novel View Synthesis via End-to-End Implicit Reconstruction and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02847)
*Xiaolei Lang,Ze Kang,Zehao Huang,Naiyan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RoGe是一个端到端统一的稀疏视图重建与视频生成框架，通过前馈重建模型构建隐式场景表征，并用目标相机射线查询得到逐视角几何特征直接注入视频扩散模型，去除了重建与生成之间的显式3D中间桥梁（如点云、3D高斯或渲染图像），实现联合训练和相互增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合方法在稀疏输入的新视角合成中结合重建与生成时，通常通过渲染图像或显式3D表示（如点图、3D高斯）作为桥梁。然而这种方式存在两个问题：1）生成模块被有损且不完美的场景投影条件化，继承了其误差；2）重建模块无法从生成中获得反馈来修正这些误差。

Method: RoGe包含两条核心设计：1）提出一种无显式3D中间表示的桥接方式——用前馈重建模型构建隐式场景表征，并通过目标相机光线进行查询获得逐视角几何特征，直接作为视频扩散模型的条件注入；2）采用重建-生成联合训练，使生成目标直接塑造其自身的几何条件，消除重建与生成之间的单向信息流。

Result: 在DL3DV数据集上，RoGe在图像级指标与视频级时间一致性上均优于重建型、生成型和混合型基线。消融实验表明，光线查询的隐式特征优于原始重建token和渲染RGB作为条件，且联合训练带来进一步增益。

Conclusion: RoGe验证了去除显式3D桥梁、通过隐式光线级特征桥接重建与生成的有效性，同时证明联合训练使两个模块能够相互增强，为稀疏视图场景漫游视频生成提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Novel view synthesis from sparse inputs requires both geometric grounding from the observed views and generative priors of unobserved regions, motivating recent hybrid methods that combine reconstruction and generation. However, existing methods bridge the two with rendered images or explicit 3D representations such as point maps or 3D Gaussians. Generation is thus conditioned on a lossy and imperfect projection of the scene, inheriting its errors, and reconstruction receives no signal from generation to correct them. We present RoGe, an end-to-end unified reconstruction and generation framework that removes this explicit bridge. It targets roaming within a scene anchored by sparse views: given a few posed images and a camera trajectory, it synthesizes a temporally coherent video along that trajectory. From the sparse input views, RoGe builds an implicit scene representation with a feed-forward reconstruction model, and queries it with target camera rays to obtain per-view geometric features. These features are injected into a video diffusion model as conditioning, without any 3D intermediate. Both modules are trained jointly, so the generation objective directly shapes its own geometric conditioning. We conduct experiments on DL3DV, where RoGe outperforms reconstruction-based, generation-based, and hybrid baselines on image-level metrics and video-level temporal consistency. Ablations confirm that ray-queried implicit features outperform both raw reconstruction tokens and rendered RGB as conditioning, and that joint training brings further gains.

</details>


### [115] [MuyBridge: Mobile Human Center-of-Mass Estimation from Monocular Video via Sparse Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02854)
*Aidan Bradshaw,Marco Giordano,David Rode,Andreas Habersack,Elif Basokur,Annika Kruse,Markus Tilp,Michele Magno,Peter Wolf,Luca Benini,Christoph Leitner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MuyBridge，一个可在手机上运行的端侧系统，仅通过单摄像头视频流即可实时估计运动员的节段质心（CoM）轨迹。该系统将紧凑2D姿态网络与蒸馏的单步单目深度网络相结合，通过基于解剖学和物理先验的解析度量融合进行标定，无需3D或任务特定监督，即可从单目视频中恢复度量级（公制）CoM轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D质心是运动、康复和临床生物力学分析的关键量，但现有方法存在两大缺陷：3D姿态跟踪和网格恢复方法仅优化3D关键点精度但缺乏解剖学约束，而多视角三角化方法需要过重的计算和采集基础设施，难以部署到CoM跟踪最有价值的实际训练与比赛场景中。因此，训练场地上教练和运动分析师难以在单摄像头条件下测量度量级CoM。

Method: MuyBridge将紧凑的2D姿态网络与蒸馏后的单步单目深度网络相结合，通过解析度量融合（analytic metric fusion）进行整合：利用解剖学和物理先验将度量级CoM锚定到图像估计中，整个过程无需任何3D监督或任务特定监督。系统支持一次性标定，采用异步的深度更新策略（2.86 Hz深度与63 FPS姿态解算），运行于iPhone 15端侧设备。

Result: 在AthletePose3D数据集（跑步、田径和花样滑冰）上进行评估，MuyBridge在一次标定下实现33-41 mm的垂直CoM误差及2.3-6.6%的绝对值相对范围误差（AbsRel），并在iPhone 15上以63 FPS姿态估计速率输出CoM估计。

Conclusion: MuyBridge首次实现了在移动端单摄像头设备上以实时帧率获得度量级、解析可约束的质心轨迹估计，使教练和运动分析师无需特殊硬件或苛刻计算即可在真实训练场景中测量CoM，填补了实验室级生物力学测量与实际端侧可部署测量之间的空白。

Abstract: The 3D center of mass (CoM) is a primary quantity in the biomechanical analysis of sport, rehabilitation, and clinical movement, yet existing 3D pose tracking, mesh recovery, and multi-view triangulation methods either optimize 3D keypoint accuracy without anatomical constraints or carry compute and capture infrastructure too heavy to deploy where CoM tracking is most useful. As a result, the metric CoM remains difficult for coaches and movement analysts to measure from a single camera where athletes train and compete. In this work, we introduce MuyBridge, an on-device system that estimates the athlete's segmental center of mass trajectory from a single phone camera video stream. MuyBridge couples a compact 2D pose network and a distilled single-step monocular depth network through an analytic metric fusion that uses anatomical and physical priors to anchor the metric CoM, requiring no 3D or task-specific supervision. Evaluated on the athletic movements of AthletePose3D (running, track and field, and figure skating), MuyBridge achieves 33-41 mm vertical CoM error and 2.3-6.6% absolute-relative range error (AbsRel) under a one-time calibration, and produces CoM estimates at the 63 FPS pose-estimation rate using asynchronous 2.86 Hz depth updates on iPhone 15. Code is available at: https://github.com/Abradshaw1/Muybridge

</details>


### [116] [PlantC2USeg: Cross-Scale Consistent Pre-Training for Few-Shot Unified Plant Point Cloud Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02860)
*Yu Tian,Xintong Jiang,Jan Franklin Adamowski,Shiv O. Prasher,Shangpeng Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PlantC2USeg是一个深度迁移学习框架，通过跨尺度一致性学习和信息受限解码策略实现植物点云分割的少样本泛化，在多个植物数据集上均达到领先性能，并验证了跨农业领域的可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代作物育种需要精确的器官级分析，但传统深度学习方法依赖大量密集标注数据，获取成本高昂。面对分布偏移（不同物种、不同传感条件）的统一点云分割任务，如何在极少额外标注下实现稳定泛化仍是一个挑战。

Method: 提出了PlantC2USeg框架，包含两个关键组件：1）跨尺度一致性学习，显式地对齐不同空间尺度间的特征；2）信息受限解码策略，防止重建捷径并促进稳健适应。预训练后支持物种间和传感条件下的少样本泛化，并通过继承阈值的统一微调进一步降低适应成本。

Result: 在Soybean3D全监督下语义IoU达91.91%、实例mWCov达94.62%；20个标注样本下分别为89.78%和90.27%；10个样本下mWCov达83.23%、IoU达83.19%。HR3D上10样本迁移至烟草、番茄和高粱平均IoU 78.41%、mWCov 79.42%；SYAU-Maize上22样本迁移IoU和mRec分别为92.75%和93.51%。ShapeNet Part上类别平均mIoU达85.0%，验证了跨农业领域的泛化能力。

Conclusion: PlantC2USeg有效减少了分布偏移下的整体适应成本，实现了可扩展的植物表型分析，并在农业领域之外展示了可迁移的3D表示学习能力。

Abstract: Modern crop breeding demands precise organ-level analysis for trait quantification, making plant point cloud segmentation (PPCS) increasingly important. However, conventional deep learning approaches rely heavily on densely annotated datasets that are labor-intensive to acquire. Unified PPCS adaptation from distribution-shifted examples with minimal additional training remains challenging. To address this, we propose PlantC2USeg, a deep transfer learning framework featuring cross-scale consistency learning to explicitly align features across spatial scales and an information-restricted decoding strategy that prevents reconstruction shortcuts and promotes robust adaptation. The resulting pre-training enables stable few-shot generalization across species and sensing conditions, while unified fine-tuning with inherited thresholds further reduces adaptation overhead. Under full supervision on Soybean3D, PlantC2USeg achieves the highest semantic IoU and instance mWCov among compared methods, at 91.91% and 94.62%. With 20 labeled samples, it leads both metrics at 89.78% and 90.27%; with only 10 samples, it retains the highest mWCov of 83.23% while achieving 83.19% IoU. Across HR3D, 10-shot transfer to tobacco, tomato, and sorghum averages 78.41% IoU and 79.42% mWCov, while 22-shot transfer to SYAU-Maize achieves the highest IoU and mRec at 92.75% and 93.51%. Furthermore, a leading category-averaged mIoU of 85.0% on ShapeNet Part demonstrates the framework's capability to handle diverse shape variations beyond agricultural domains. These results demonstrate that PlantC2USeg reduces overall adaptation effort under distribution shifts, enabling scalable plant phenotyping and transferable 3D representation learning beyond agriculture.

</details>


### [117] [Thinking in Pictures: A Systematic Benchmark for Reasoning-driven Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02864)
*Yutong Liu,Nan Huang,Xu Cao,James M. Rehg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RIG-BENCH基准测试，系统评估统一生成模型（UGMs）和世界模拟器在"推理驱动图像生成"（Reasoning-to-Generation）领域的表现，涵盖概念、变换、模式与结构、场景四大认知领域共计2000个样本，揭示了当前模型普遍存在的"推理-生成差距"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一生成模型虽在视觉感知与合成上取得了显著进展，但主要依赖表面级事件对齐，缺乏从视觉输入中推断潜在规则并通过精确、逻辑受约束的视觉输出进行推理生成（Reasoning-to-Generation）的高层视觉推理能力，这一能力尚待系统性探索和评估。

Method: 作者构建了RIG-BENCH基准，包含2000个精心策划的样本，覆盖四个认知要求较高的领域：基于概念的推理、基于变换的推理、模式与结构推理以及基于场景的推理，并对当前最先进的UGMs及图像/视频生成模型进行全面评估。

Result: 对当前最先进的UGMs和图像/视频生成模型的大规模评估揭示了显著的"推理-生成差距"：模型经常产生局部合理但整体不合逻辑的输出。

Conclusion: RIG-BENCH作为关键的诊断框架，有助于指导下一代具备逻辑基础的UGMs和世界模拟器的开发。

Abstract: Recent advancements in unified generative models (UGMs) and world simulators have achieved unprecedented results in visual perception and synthesis. However, these models primarily rely on surface-level event alignment, leaving the capacity for high-level visual reasoning underexplored. True visual generative intelligence demands "Reasoning-to-Generation", an ability to infer latent rules from visual inputs and manifest solutions through precise, logically constrained visual outcomes. We introduce RIG-BENCH, a novel comprehensive benchmark that systematically evaluates Reasoning-driven Image Generation (RIG) across four cognitively demanding domains: Concept-based, Transformation-based, Pattern & Structure, and Scenario-based. Featuring 2000 curated samples, RIG-BENCH serves as a rigorous stress test for RIG. Our extensive evaluations of state-of-the-art UGMs and image/video generation models reveal a significant reasoning-generation gap, wherein models frequently produce locally plausible but globally illogical outputs. RIG-BENCH provides a vital diagnostic framework to guide the development of next-generation, logically grounded UGMs and world simulators.

</details>


### [118] [SolarWM: Open Data and Scalable Training for Long-Horizon Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02886)
*Junchao Huang,Guian Fang,Shengju Qian,Xianghao Kong,Zhuoran Zhao,Wei Huang,Yihua Du,Zixin Zhang,Justin Cui,Yuchao Gu,Yukang Chen,Xinting Hu,Tianyu He,Shaoshuai Shi,Zhuotao Tian,Xin Wang,Mike Zheng Shou,Li Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SolarWM 是一个完全开源的基础框架，用于构建交互式视频世界模型，涵盖从数据准备到长时程推理的完整流程。它通过可重构的多源数据引擎和骨干网络原生适配框架，将10个数据集中的143万个规范剪辑统一为帧对齐的数据契约，并基于Wan2.2、LTX-2.5和MiniMax-H3构建了四个5B-33B的因果模型，使训练于5秒序列的模型能够实现分钟到小时级别的实时交互滚动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构数据源（在时间尺度、相机几何、视觉质量、运动和字幕风格上各不相同）与不同的视频生成骨干网络（使用不同表示和架构）之间高度耦合，导致简单的数据混合和特定模型实现产生不一致的监督信号，且结果难以复现和公平比较。作者希望解耦数据处理与模型训练，提供一个通用且可复现的框架。

Method: SolarWM提出两个核心组件：(1) 可重构的多源数据引擎，将来自10个数据集的143万个规范剪辑转换为统一的帧对齐数据契约（涵盖视觉观测、度量相机几何、字幕、质量元数据、选择决策和出处），解耦源处理与混合构建；(2) 骨干网络原生适配框架，在共享的相机调节、训练和推理接口下，基于Wan2.2、LTX-2.5和MiniMax-H3实例化四个5B-33B模型，保持其原生表示和目标函数。采用统一的三阶段训练策略：双向适应、教师强制的自回归初始化以及分布匹配蒸馏，最终得到支持实时交互的因果模型。

Result: SolarWM基于仅5秒的训练序列，成功构建了支持从分钟到小时级别的长时程滚动、具备实时交互能力的因果视频世界模型。所有数据、管道、训练配方、模型权重和框架均被完整开源，提供了可复现且可扩展的基础设施。

Conclusion: SolarWM通过解耦数据与模型构建的耦合问题，为交互式视频世界模型研究提供了一个统一、可复现且可扩展的完全开源基础框架，使得异构数据和多种骨干网络能够在一个标准化的接口下协同工作。

Abstract: We introduce SolarWM, a fully open foundation for building interactive video world models from data preparation through long-horizon inference. Training across heterogeneous data sources and video backbones is challenging: datasets differ in temporal scale, camera geometry, visual quality, motion, and captioning styles, while video generators use distinct representations and architectures. Naive data mixing and model-specific implementations therefore produce inconsistent supervision and make results difficult to reproduce and compare. SolarWM addresses this coupling with a reconfigurable multi-source data engine and a backbone-native adaptation framework. The engine converts 1.43 million canonical clips from 10 datasets into a unified, frame-aligned contract covering visual observations, metric camera geometry, captions, quality metadata, selection decisions, and provenance, while decoupling source processing from mixture construction. Under shared camera-conditioning, training, and inference interfaces, we instantiate four 5B--33B models based on Wan2.2, LTX-2.5, and MiniMax-H3 while preserving their native representations and objectives. A unified three-stage recipe combines bidirectional adaptation, teacher-forced autoregressive initialization, and distribution matching distillation. The resulting causal models enable real-time interaction over rollouts ranging from minutes to hours after being trained on only 5s sequences. By releasing the resulting data, pipeline, recipes, weights, and framework, SolarWM provides a reproducible and extensible foundation for interactive world-model research.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [PRO-Step: Step-level Process Reward Optimization for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01658)
*MinKeon Kim,Namjun Lee,Jaekwang Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PRO-STEP是一个面向检索增强生成（RAG）多跳推理的逐步训练框架。它通过训练生成式过程奖励模型（PRM）同时评估每一步的逻辑有效性和证据支撑，利用PRM引导的值树搜索构建偏好对，并采用逐步直接偏好优化（DPO）来优化策略，从而解决RAG多跳推理中错误传播和虚假成功的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG通过外部知识增强了大语言模型，但多跳推理容易遭受错误传播：早期的检索失败会影响后续步骤。标准的基于结果的优化仅奖励最终答案，无法检测中间检索和推理错误；已有的过程方法虽然引入步骤级信号，但仍然以最终答案为标准对每步打分，会奖励那些检索有缺陷但碰巧得到正确答案的虚假成功步骤。因此需要同时评估每一步的逻辑有效性和证据支撑。

Method: 作者提出PRO-STEP框架，包含三个核心部分：(1) 训练一个生成式过程奖励模型(PRM)，用于评估RAG过程中每个步骤的逻辑有效性和证据支撑两个维度；(2) 使用PRM引导的值树搜索(Value Tree Search)来构建偏好对，将有效的推理步骤与有缺陷的步骤进行对比；(3) 通过步骤级直接偏好优化(step-level DPO)来优化策略模型。

Result: 在单跳和多跳问答数据集的五个基准上，PRO-STEP取得了最优的平均EM（Exact Match）和F1分数。

Conclusion: PRO-STEP通过引入双维度步骤级监督（逻辑有效性与证据支撑），结合过程奖励模型引导的偏好优化，有效改善了RAG多跳推理的性能，代码、模型和训练数据均已公开。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation enhances Large Language Models by grounding responses in external knowledge, but multi-hop reasoning remains vulnerable to error propagation, where early retrieval failures confound subsequent steps. Standard outcome-based optimization only rewards the final answer, leaving intermediate retrieval and reasoning errors undetected. While existing process-based methods introduce step-level signals, they still score each step against the final answer, rewarding spurious successes where flawed retrieval coincidentally produces the correct answer. Step-level supervision in RAG requires evaluating both logical validity and evidential grounding at each step. We introduce PRO-STEP: we train a generative PRM that evaluates both dimensions, employ PRM-guided value tree search to construct preference pairs contrasting valid steps against flawed ones, and optimize the policy via step-level Direct Preference Optimization. Experiments on single and multi-hop QA datasets demonstrate that PRO-STEP achieves the best average EM and F1 across five benchmarks. Code, models, and training data are publicly available at https://github.com/keemminnke/PRO-Step.

</details>


### [120] [Learning Evidence Sufficiency Boundaries for Selective Answering in Grounded Multi-Hop QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01687)
*Haruto Sato,Yuki Tanaka,Ren Nakamura,Aoi Kobayashi,Mei Ito*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出证据充分性边界训练（ESBT），一种面向生成式模型的训练框架，通过构建有序证据链并监督"弃答到作答"的转换，使多跳QA系统仅在证据充分时作答，实验表明其在边界定位和降低无证据支持作答率上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多跳问答系统中，接地回答要求仅在证据支持答案时才作答，但部分证据可能使不支持的答案看起来很合理，导致系统在证据不足或部分支持时错误作答。现有方法难以精确判断证据何时从不足变为充分，因此需要一种能够显式建模"证据充分性边界"的训练方法。

Method: 提出证据充分性边界训练（ESBT），一种生成原生的训练框架：1）从HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA和MuSiQue构建有序证据链；2）结合层级监督（level supervision）、边界翻转边际（boundary flip margin）、边界后稳定性（post-boundary stability）和答案召回保护（answer recall protection）；3）在Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct上使用LoRA进行微调，直接监督从弃答到作答的转换。

Result: ESBT在边界定位上达到最强性能，翻转准确率0.807（优于token级弃答基线的0.781）；在外部不可回答数据集上具有最低的无支持作答率0.095（基线为0.101）；同时保持竞争力的raw QA F1分数。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，当训练过程显式标记"拒绝作答应让位于作答"的证据层级时，接地选择性问答的性能会得到显著提升，验证了通过证据充分性边界训练来改善多跳QA中证据充分性判断的有效性。

Abstract: Grounded question answering systems should answer only when the supplied evidence supports the answer. In multi-hop QA, this requirement is difficult because partial evidence can make an unsupported answer appear plausible. We study selective answering through evidence sufficiency boundaries: for the same question, a model should abstain under unsupported or partially supported context, answer when the context first becomes sufficient, and keep the answer stable when redundant evidence is added. We introduce Evidence Sufficiency Boundary Training, a generation-native training framework that constructs ordered evidence chains and supervises the abstain-to-answer transition directly. The method combines level supervision, a boundary flip margin, post-boundary stability, and answer recall protection. We build evidence chains from HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and MuSiQue, then evaluate models with chain metrics, raw QA utility, and unsupported-answer rates on external non-answerable sets. With Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct and LoRA adaptation, Evidence Sufficiency Boundary Training gives the strongest boundary localization among the tested systems, with flip accuracy of 0.807 compared with 0.781 for a token-level abstention baseline. It also achieves the lowest overall unsupported-answer rate on external non-answerable evaluation, 0.095 compared with 0.101 for the same baseline, while retaining competitive raw QA F1. The results show that grounded selective answering improves when training marks the evidence level where refusal should give way to answering.

</details>


### [121] [SpeakPay: Domain-Adaptive LoRA Fine-Tuning of Whisper for Low-Resource Nepali Financial Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01737)
*Biraj Subedi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文介绍SpeakPay——一个面向尼泊尔视障用户的语音优先数字钱包，核心贡献是针对低资源金融语音识别的领域自适应受控研究。作者构建了NepFinSpeech-403（403条尼泊尔金融语音指令数据集），并通过LoRA微调Whisper large-v2，将词错误率从129.95%降至42.58%（相对降低67.2%），Devanagari数字识别准确率从0%提升至73.9%，交易成功率提升约20倍。系统已部署为公开可访问的语音优先Web应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尼泊尔的移动支付应用以图形界面为主，对视障用户极为不友好。尽管语音优先的数字钱包具有明显的无障碍潜力，但在尼泊尔语等低资源语言环境下，金融领域的语音识别面临着训练数据匮乏、数字识别准确率低等严峻挑战，现有研究缺乏对这一领域的系统探索。

Method: 论文采用LoRA（低秩自适应）技术对Whisper large-v2模型进行领域微调，并构建了NepFinSpeech-403数据集——包含403条尼泊尔金融语音指令（发送、充值、余额查询三种操作，涵盖237个唯一数字）。通过受控实验评估领域自适应的效果，包括字级指标（WER）、任务级指标（交易成功率）和数据效率分析，并运用符号检验进行统计显著性验证。

Result: 领域自适应模型将WER从129.95%显著降至42.58%（相对降低67.2%），Devanagari数字识别准确率从0.0%提升至73.9%。更重要的是任务级改进：交易成功率从1.67%提升至33.33%（约20倍提升），个体语料级提升具有统计显著性（p<1e-17）。数据效率分析显示100条领域语音足以将零样本WER减半，性能在约300条示例时趋于饱和。误差分析揭示了系统性的数字混淆模式（零的插入/删除、前缀幻觉）导致多数剩余交易失败。

Conclusion: 字级指标会低估领域自适应的实际任务级影响。即使在极低资源条件下（100-300条领域语音），轻量化微调（LoRA）也能为低资源语言的金融语音识别带来实质性改善，为无障碍移动支付系统提供了可行的技术路径。所有代码、数据集、模型权重均已开源发布。

Abstract: Mobile payment applications in Nepal are graphically mediated and largely inaccessible to visually impaired users. This paper presents SpeakPay, a voice-first digital wallet, and documents the central technical contribution: a controlled study of domain adaptation for low-resource financial speech recognition. We introduce NepFinSpeech-403, a 403-utterance dataset of Nepali financial voice commands (send, load, and balance operations spanning 237 unique numerals), and fine-tune Whisper large-v2 with LoRA. On the held-out test set, the domain-adapted model reduces Word Error Rate from 129.95% (zero-shot baseline) to 42.58% --- a 67.2% relative reduction --- and improves Devanagari numeral recognition accuracy from 0.0% to 73.9%. We find that word-level metrics understate the practical task-level impact: domain adaptation improves the Transaction Success Rate from 1.67% to 33.33%, a roughly 20x gain. The improvement is consistent at the individual-utterance level (sign test, $p < 10^{-17}$) and across all command types. A data efficiency analysis shows that as few as 100 domain-specific utterances are sufficient to halve the zero-shot WER, with performance plateauing around 300 examples. Error analysis reveals systematic numeral confusion patterns (zero insertion/deletion, prefix hallucination) that account for the majority of remaining transaction failures. The trained system is deployed as a publicly accessible voice-first web application. All code, dataset, model weights, and this paper are released at https://github.com/subedibiraj/speakpay.

</details>


### [122] [MemeCULT-1K: Benchmarking South Asian Cultural Context and Humor Understanding of Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01772)
*Tawsif Tashwar Dipto,Mehedi Ahamed,Radib Bin Kabir,Mueeze Al Mushabbir,Mohammed Saidul Islam,Mir Rayat Imtiaz Hossain,Md Tahmid Rahman Laskar,Sabbir Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MemeCULT-1K，一个包含1,000个南亚表情包（孟加拉语、英语、印地语）及文化背景注释的三语基准数据集，评估了13个视觉语言模型在有无文化上下文两种设置下的多模态理解能力，发现提供文化上下文能显著提升所有模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表情包理解不仅需要识别视觉内容或字面文本，还依赖于隐含的文化知识和语用推理，而现有视觉语言模型普遍缺乏这种能力。作者旨在创建一个包含区域性文化背景的多语表情包基准，以系统性地评估和揭示这些模型的不足。

Method: 构建MemeCULT-1K基准，包含1,000个分别来自孟加拉语、英语和印地语的南亚表情包，每个表情包配有文化背景注释和三段人工撰写的解释；额外补充54个孟加拉地区方言表情包。在meme-only和context-aware两种设置下评估13个流行VLM，使用SBERT、BLEURT和LLM-as-judge三个指标进行打分，并进行了细粒度错误分析。

Result: 提供最小化的文化上下文在所有模型和语言上都带来一致增益：SBERT相似度从44.6提升到56.4（+11.8），BLEURT从37.3提升到42.3（+5.0），LLM-as-a-Judge评分从2.57/5提升到3.43/5（+0.86）。细粒度错误分析显示：闭源模型主要失败于实体和指代误识别，开源模型受限于更广泛的文化知识缺口，而语言和音韵层面的失败对两类模型最具上下文抗性。

Conclusion: 研究凸显了文化根源性表情包理解的困难，表明单纯增加上下文提示不足以完全解决语言/音韵层面的文化理解障碍，激励了未来在显式文化知识融入方面的研究。数据集和代码已公开。

Abstract: Meme understanding goes beyond recognizing visual content or literal text; it requires implicit cultural knowledge and pragmatic inference that most vision-language models still lack. We introduce MemeCULT-1K, a multilingual benchmark of 1,000 South Asian memes in Bengali, English, and Hindi, where each meme is paired with a cultural context note and three human-written explanations, along with a supplementary set of 54 Bengali regional dialect memes. We evaluate thirteen popular Vision Language Models (VLMs) under two settings: meme-only and context-aware. Providing minimal cultural context yields consistent gains across all models and languages: mean SBERT similarity improves from 44.6 to 56.4 (+11.8), BLEURT from 37.3 to 42.3 (+5.0), and LLM-as-a-Judge scores from 2.57 to 3.43 out of 5 (+0.86). Fine-grained error analysis reveals that closed-source models fail mainly on entity and reference misidentification, while open-source models are bottlenecked by broader cultural knowledge gaps, with linguistic and phonological failures proving the most context-resistant across both. These results highlight the difficulty of culturally grounded meme understanding and motivate future work on explicit cultural knowledge integration. Our dataset and code are publicly available at TawsifDipto17/MemeCULT-1K.

</details>


### [123] [VakyArth: Evaluating Pragmatic Competence in LLMs across Indic Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01788)
*Usneek Singh,Poorvaja Veera Balaji Kumar,Parth Nanda,Anand Madhusoodanan,Geyang Guo,Wei Xu,Junyi Jessy L*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了VakyArth，这是首个面向印度语言的语用学基准测试，覆盖印地语、旁遮普语、泰米尔语和马拉雅拉姆语四种语言，评估五大语用现象（指示语、言语行为、含义推断、社交语用和连贯性）。实验发现多语言大语言模型在印度语言的语用理解上系统性失败，MCQ准确率普遍高于NLI，翻译指标无法可靠反映语用理解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语用推理评估主要局限于英语和高资源语言，印度语言因其丰富的语言和文化多样性却未被探索。真实世界交流通常依赖语用推理（通过上下文和文化惯例理解隐含意义），因此亟需一个系统性基准来诊断多语言大语言模型在印度语言上的语用理解能力。

Method: 构建VakyArth基准，涵盖印地语、旁遮普语（印欧语系）和泰米尔语、马拉雅拉姆语（达罗毗荼语系）四种印度语言，覆盖指示语、言语行为、含义推断、社交语用和连贯性五类语用现象，采用多项选择题（MCQ）、自然语言推理（NLI）和翻译三种任务形式，所有题目由母语者编写。对多种多语言大语言模型进行系统性评估和分析。
